- 07.09.26 03:05 [E-1677] [parent: E-1676] [T-1221] [agent: opencode] [op: checkpoint-recover-t1221] RUN: RECOVERED CURRENT IMPLEMENTATION -- T-1221 scheduler already in source, preserved: no semantic SoundManager._pending FIFO for ordinary requests (deque exists but nothing appends to it); STACK_SHORT/COALESCE_HIGH_RATE/EXCLUSIVE_LONG/PREVIEW policies; explicit dedupe_key coalesce gate in _request; immediate short-over-alarm REPLACED_ALARM intent; QSoundEffect fresh-player-per-short with previous short stopped; SND_ASYNC short winsound support (sync flag in _play_winsound/_winsound_play); SND_SYNC long playback worker thread; bounded provenance ring 256; 12-frame cached caller provenance; Ctrl+Q rapid-repeat tests (36x). VERIFIED REMAINING DEFECT: _SerialWavWorker.jobs is an unbounded queue.Queue and EXCLUSIVE_LONG jobs play sync=True, so the worker blocks for the whole long WAV and every later request FIFOs behind it; _stop_exclusive_alarms silences the device but never purges jobs, so hidden transport intent survives a semantic cancel; _pending empty does NOT imply transport empty. Second defect: QSoundEffect EXCLUSIVE_LONG path overwrites _players['__ui__'] without stopping the previous long player (chorus risk). Plan: failing packaged-backend regressions first (2.1-2.5), then latest-wins single-slot worker mailbox + immediate caller-thread SND_ASYNC shorts + one _preempt_current_transport helper + QSoundEffect long REPLACE. - 07.09.26 04:04 [E-1678] [parent: E-1677] [T-1221] [agent: opencode] [op: checkpoint-t1221-done] RUN: T-1221 DONE -- ROOT CAUSE (two, discovered on the real device): (1) winsound's module lock serializes ALL PlaySound calls, so a SND_SYNC long holds the lock for the whole WAV and every later dispatch -- including the interrupt PlaySound(None, 0) -- waits behind it (measured: a stop waited 9010ms behind a 9.5s alarm; that lock, not semantic _pending, is the user's actual FIFO replay report); (2) winsound has NO SND_SYNC constant, so _winsound_play's winsound.SND_SYNC raised AttributeError on real Windows and every long alarm on the packaged path silently FAILED (never played at all). FIX: _SerialWavWorker rebuilt as a latest-wins single-slot mailbox (Condition + one _next_job + epoch; jobs.put FIFO removed; holding-token exposes the taken job); longs dispatch SND_ASYNC and wait out their file duration on the worker WITHOUT holding the device lock (interruptible by submit notify / drop_pending / close); worker pre-start silence + epoch re-check discards superseded intent before it is heard; caller replaces mailbox FIRST then silences, plus a bounded holding-token confirm loop closing the start-vs-stop race; shorts dispatch SND_ASYNC directly on the caller thread (never enter the worker; scaled-cache kept, first-use scale bounded+cached); _preempt_current_transport(discard_pending, silence) is the single preemption owner (shorts also drop parked long intent); QSoundEffect EXCLUSIVE_LONG now REPLACES (stops previous long player, no chorus); stale completion token invariant preserved; _winsound_play fixed to flags=SND_FILENAME|SND_NODEFAULT(+SND_ASYNC when async) since SND_SYNC does not exist. TESTS: uv run pytest tests/test_sound_manager.py -q -> 80 passed (12 new packaged-transport regressions: 2.1 long replacement no-FIFO, 2.2 A/B/C latest-wins, 2.3 short-during-long + parked-intent drop, 2.4 36-press burst 36 dispatches, 2.5 shutdown interrupt+bounded+idempotent, dedupe-never-stops-current, same-file-without-dedupe distinct, stale-completion harmless, transport-FIFO language); 214 passed across 16 sound-caller suites; compileall PASS; ruff clean. WINDOWS (real device, packaged branch forced QSoundEffect=None, dev checkout -- EXE itself not built, no release code touched): A long replacement 0ms; B click-during-long 11.6ms (was 9010ms); C 20 rapid presses 1ms total, no replay; D newest alarm owns device 0-5ms, 3 older cancelled; E exit during long: worker terminated 7ms, bounded join, repeated shutdown safe. Two runs identical. T-1216 supersede note in E-1679. STOP: no release work in this context.

- 29.09.26 16:45 [E-3482] [parent: E-3481] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: build] BUILD: ONE single-shot QTimer per VaultTextEdit (INLINE_HOVER_GRACE_MS=200) bridges the dead spatial gap in TIME, not space. _update_inline_hover order now token > copy/bridge zone > grace > clear. Grace arms only on a real leave, never re-arms from its own timeout, and is killed by _clear_inline_hover so a cleared control can never be resurrected.
- 29.09.26 16:50 [E-3483] [parent: E-3482] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: build] BUILD: click authority hardened -- _link_copy_at/_image_copy_at now refuse through _inline_hover_still_valid() (token still in the CURRENT block, image pills still painted, view mode unchanged). contentsChange revalidation in _reconcile_edits drops the control the moment an edit deletes the token; preview_combo.currentIndexChanged and the scroll re-hover both drop it too.
- 29.09.26 16:55 [E-3484] [parent: E-3483] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: build] BUILD: the "below the QTextBlock is guaranteed text-free" assumption is GONE. _copy_rect_inside_viewport now gates BOTH axes; the fallback tries a slot below/above the TARGET's own row proven free against the previous, current and next visible blocks (_neighbour_text_rects), then a bounded viewport-overlay strip scanned inward from the far edge. Never off-screen, never covers the target, never moves document text.
- 29.09.26 17:00 [E-3485] [parent: E-3484] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: test] RUN: matrix A..L added on top of the 3 recovered cases. - 29.09.26 17:04 [E-3487] [parent: E-3486] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: test] DEC: test_t1337::test_leaving_clears_the_copy_control encoded the instant kill T-1349 supersedes; rewritten to assert the bounded grace and its expiry. Preserved T-1337 list is untouched.
- 29.09.26 17:07 [E-3488] [parent: E-3487] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: test] FIND: first full tests/ run failed 4 T-1349 cases that pass in isolation. - 29.09.26 17:09 [E-3489] [parent: E-3488] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: test] BUILD: _force_hard_placement measures and narrows until the link's row is full to the edge, so "no premium slot exists" is proven at whatever font the suite left installed. - 29.09.26 17:24 [E-3490] [parent: E-3489] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: build] RUN: FIND+FIX: a real product defect the second full run exposed -- the overlay fallback could land ON its own target, because _row_text_extents excludes the target on purpose. The target is now a blocker for every fallback step, so the search prefers a spot clear of the token. Step 6 (last resort) keeps a fully bounded far-edge rect and its comment now says the overlap honestly instead of pretending it cannot happen.
- 29.09.26 17:25 [E-3491] [parent: E-3490] [T-1349] [agent: buffy] [op: test] DEC: the bottom-viewport case asserts the S9 boundedness contract only. DEC on the one genuine engineering choice: T-1357's hardened release_provenance.py IS in the release-critical file set (1496 files), so folding the fingerprint check into 0.8.69 would change the tree fingerprint, invalidate the operator-accepted EXE and force a fresh human acceptance. Conventional answer: ship 0.8.69 as accepted, land T-1357 for 0.8.70. The fingerprint is therefore verified out of band, not merely asserted

- 30.09.26 18:12 [E-3666] [parent: E-3665] [T-1351] [agent: buffy] [op: fix-release-provenance-git-check-false] RUN: BUILD: tools/release_provenance.py git() returned proc.stdout even when the command failed, because check=False suppresses the exception but not the exit status. 'git rev-parse v0.8.69^{commit}' echoes the literal unresolved argument on stdout and exits 128; release.py check_tag_provenance read that echo as a real tag at a different commit and refused to publish the FIRST release of a version that was never tagged, with the misleading message 'version already tagged at different commit; bump VERSION'. Fix: return empty on non-zero exit after the check=True raise. 2 regression tests in tests/test_release_provenance.py -- one asserts git('rev-parse', 'v9.9.9^{commit}', check=False) is empty in a throwaway repo, one asserts check_tag_provenance no longer raises for an untagged version while still raising when the tag exists elsewhere; ablation: 2 fail without the fix, 9 pass with it. Landed for 0.8.70 (see E-3664: release_provenance.py is release-critical, so it cannot enter 0.8.69)
